And, you know, I guess I did blurt things out before they finished asking a question. But... yeah, it's been a while.\n\nDoctor: Have these difficulties with focus and restlessness caused you significant distress or problems in your daily life?\n\nPatient: I mean... I'm still keeping up overall. It's not like everything is falling apart. But... I'm managing.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
